Ngā Ariā
 
 Here-turi-kōkā 2026 
 
 
 
Ko ngā tuhinga i raro nei he rekoata haere tonu o ngā whakaaro me ngā kitenga i puta mai ki a au i roto i te wā. Kāore i te tino oti, kāore hoki i te tino whakatutukihia, ahakoa ka taea pea ā muri ake nei. Tērā pea kāore rānei, he nui noa atu te puka tuhipoka mō ētahi whakaaro. Ko ētahi he huarahi kāore e puta, nō reira kāore he take e whakawhanake tonu ai. Engari ahakoa tērā, he mea pai tonu te tuhi iho.

 2024-08-19:  TE POETI A KUSHÍK

 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
I hangaia e au tētahi hōtaka mō taku kāmera wera Seek Thermal RevealPRO ki te tiro me te whakatika i ngā whakaahua wera. Kāore au i kite i tētahi pūmanawa e tika ana mō tēnei kāmera i runga i te Windows, he mea pōuri tērā, nā te mea kei roto i āna kōnae wera ngā raraunga pāmahana e taea ana te whakamahi tonu.
 
I tapaina e au te hōtaka ko Thermozepam, nā te mea e mārie ana ahau ki tōna hoahoa, ā, he mea whakahīhī te whakamahi i a ia mōku. Ka utaina e te hōtaka ngā kōnae TIFF wera, ka taea te huri i te mahere tae o te whakaahua mā te whakamahi i te colormap mai i te whare pukapuka matplotlib, te ine i te pāmahana toharite o te whakaahua, te whakatau i te aratau pāmahana, te whakaatu i te pāmahana iti rawa me te pāmahana teitei rawa, te histogram, ā, i muri i te pāwhiri, te pāmahana o tētahi pika motuhake. I te wā e tiaki ana, ka taea e ia te kaweake i tētahi kōnae PNG kua maherehia anō te tae, ā, ki te hiahia, ka taea te whakanui mā te whakamahi i te interpolation.

 2025-12-05:  TE KANAPU I TE PŌURI
 
I whakaaro au ki te hanga i tētahi kaupapa Arduino, e whakatakoto ana au i tētahi laser ki roto i tētahi pouaka kau (i horoia i mua mā te rama UV-C). Ka pupuhi te laser i tētahi hēkona i tētahi wā ohorere (kia kore ai au e mōhio āhea te wā e tupu ai, engari kia mōhio au ka tupu pea i roto i ngā 10 meneti i te 100%). Mēnā i taea e au te mahi tika, kāore tētahi kaititiro e kite i taua kanapu o te māramatanga.
 
 2026-01-31:  WHAKATAUIRA KĀMERA PINHOLE
 
I whakamātau au ki te hanga i tētahi kāmera pinhole māmā e mahi ana i roto i te Python. I mua i te kura tuarua i hanga kē au i tētahi kāmera tūturu, engari i whakaaro au mēnā ka whakatauira au i a ia, ka pai ake taku mārama ki tōna mahi, nā te mea kāore e ranea te hanga noa i te kōhao ki te pouaka, te whakauru i te pepa tairongo ki te māramatanga, te whakawhanake i a ia i muri i te whakaaturanga me te tuku i te ahupūngao ki te mahi i tana mahi (ahakoa pēhea tōna mahi). I konei, me whakatauira hoki te ahupūngao.
 I ngana au ki te hanga i te whakatauira kia tino tūturu te āhua o te hōtaka, ahakoa ko te rohe ko ōku mōhiotanga me te mana rorohiko e wātea ana. Hei tauira, ka whakamahi te hōtaka i te forward ray tracing, arā, te whai i te māramatanga mai i te pūtake ki te whakaaturanga me te kāmera, kaua ko te backward ray tracing e whakamahia ana, e whai ana i ngā hihi mai i te kāmera ki te whakaaturanga. Ko te huarahi forward he tata ake ki te huarahi tūturu o te horapa o te māramatanga, engari he tino kore e whai hua i runga i te rorohiko.
 
Ngā tauira o te tautuhinga hōtaka me te whakamārama o ngā tawhā: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]

 2026-03-02:  TE WHAKARŌPŪ PIKA O NGĀ WHAKAAHUA
 
I whakamātau au ki te whakarōpū i ngā pika o ngā whakaahua mā te whakamahi i tētahi hōtaka. Ko te whakaaro he whakarōpū i ngā pika i runga i te HUE me te māramatanga: ki tētahi tuaka (pēnei i te x) whakarōpū i ngā pika i runga i te HUE, ā, ki tētahi atu tuaka (pēnei i te y) i runga i te māramatanga. Mā tēnei ka whakaritea anō ngā pika o te whakaahua, engari kāore tētahi e ngaro, kāore hoki he mea hou e puta. Heoi, ka ngaro te tikanga taketake o ngā whakaahua i puta mai, engari ka mau tonu te āhuarangi tae.

 2026-04-13:  TE TOROHANGA I TE IPURANGI I runga i te Ohorere
 
E rua wiki pea au e torohanga ana i te ipurangi i runga i te ohorere, nā te mea i hiahia au ki te mōhio he aha te āhua o te ipurangi mēnā kāore e tūtohutia ana e ngā hōtaka. I whakamātau au i ngā huarahi rerekē.
 
Ko te tuatahi (te mea māmā) ko te hanga i tētahi hōtaka hei whakauru me te whakatuwhera i ngā URL e hangaia ana i runga i te ohorere. He tino kore e whai hua tērā, he nui rawa te wāhi o ngā URL e taea ana, ā, he iti noa ngā mea e noho ana.
 
Ko te huarahi tuarua ko te hanga i tōku ake crawler me te hoatu i tētahi URL kākano rerekē (pēnei i te paetukutuku kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). I ngana au ki te tātari i ngā hua mā te whakamahi i te LLM, ā, i muri iho ka titiro noa ki ngā mea e ngahau ana (i runga i ētahi paearu i tautuhia). He maha ngā mea rerekē i kitea e au hei whakangahau, engari kāore he mea nui.
 
Ko te huarahi tuatoru he nui ake te hua me te ngahau. I kitea e au te Certstream. He ratonga pai tēnei e pāho ora ana i ngā tiwhikete haumaru e tukuna ana i te ao katoa. Nō reira, e whakaatu ana i ngā URL o ngā paetukutuku e hou ana, e whakahou ana rānei i tō rātou tiwhikete.
 
Waehere pūtake: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
I pau ētahi rā i a au e pāwhiri ana i ēnei URL i runga i te ohorere. Ko te nuinga o ngā wā i tūpono au ki ngā mahi tinihanga (cryptocurrency, etc.), porn, AI, AI porn, ngā tohutohu mō te hanga AI porn, petipeti, ngā atanga takiuru o ngā pūnaha, ngā paetukutuku e whānau mai ana (he placeholder te ihirangi, he kōrero rānei pēnei i te "e mahi ana mātou i tēnei").
 
Nā tēnei tukanga i tūpono au ki te paetukutuku treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], i mau tonu ki tōku mahara nā tōna āhua māmā me te kore o te arotautanga mō te aro o te kaimātakitaki. I kitea hoki e au he wāhanga taha noa tēnei o te ipurangi, nā te mea kāore anō a Google i tohu i a ia. I kitea e te Internet Archive, engari mai i ngā CT logs, pēnei i a au. I whai hua te rapu i te mutunga.
 
 2026-05-06:  WHAKAATURANGA I TE PASIFIKA
 
I whakaaro au ki te hanga i ngā whakaahua panorāmika 360° o te Pasifika i hangaia mā te AI. Kātahi ka whakauru i tētahi QR code ki roto mā te AI (he mea pēnei i te Illusion Diffusion), e tohutohu ana ki tētahi mahi toi. I muri iho, ka taea te whakauru i ngā metadata tūnga teka (pēnei i ngā GPS coordinates i waenganui o te Pasifika) me te tuku ki Google Maps.
 
Hei kōwhiringa, ka taea te tango i ētahi wāhi tūturu i whakaahuatia e te kāmera 360°, pēnei i tētahi tāone, kātahi ka nuku i ō rātou tūnga GPS ki te Pasifika. Ko te take i kōrero ai au mō ngā whakaahua 360° anake, nā te mea e whakaae tonu ana a Google ki te tuku i a rātou ki ngā tūnga GPS tika mā te whakamahi i ngā metadata. I a ngā whakaahua noa me tohu ki tētahi wāhi e noho ana.
 
 2026-06-04:  NGĀ MATE KIRI
 
I ngana au ki te whakatutuki i tēnei kaupapa, engari nā te roa o te wā i hiahiatia, kāore au i whakaoti. I rekoatatia e au ōku ringa anake, i tapahia ngā pī i whakaahuatia (papamuri mārama), i mau tonu tō rātou tauine, ā, i hanga i tētahi hōtaka hei whakarite i a rātou ki te mātiti. Heoi, ko tēnei te tuhinga i whakaaro ai au ki te whakamārama i te ariā:
 
“I te tau 2026 i rekoatatia e au ia puku kiri, ia rerekētanga uaua rānei i runga i te mata o tōku tinana. I mau tonu te tauine mō ia mea. I muri iho, i tapahia ngā mate kiri takitahi ki te matihiko, ā, i whakaritea ki te mātiti auau.”
 
 2026-06-21:  HŌTAKA WHAKAMŌHIO E WHAKATIKA ANA I A IA ANO
 
I whakamātau au ki te hanga i tētahi hōtaka e whakamahi ana i te OpenAI API me ā rātou LLM. Ko te mahi a te hōtaka he whakatika i a ia anō, kātahi ka whakahaere anō. Ā, i hiahia au ki te mōhio, ka pēhea te mutunga.
 Heoi, i whakakore te tauira i te mahi i te nuinga o te wā, nā te mea i whakaaro ia he mōrearea. Rānei i tutuki i a ia, engari kāore i taea e tana kaiwhaiwhai. Ko te mea i puta tonu ko te whakakore nā te haumaru. Kāore hoki ia i tae ki te iterati tuawhā.

 2026-07-30:  PŪNAHA ME NGĀ WĀHANGA MOTUHAKE
 
He hōhā tō tātou pūnaha ahupūngao i te mea e āhua rite ana ngā wāhanga tūturu ki a rātou anō. Ko te irahiko he irahiko, ko te foton he foton.
 
Engari e hiahia ana au ki te hanga i tētahi whakatauira o tētahi pūnaha he maha ngā wāhanga tūturu, engari kāore he tokorua o rātou e taea te whakawhiti. I te mutunga i tae au ki tētahi rerekētanga, kāore he āhuatanga, he tuakiri rānei o ngā wāhanga i a rātou anō. E tautuhia ana rātou e ō rātou pāhekoheko ki a rātou anō, he mea e rite ana ki te tirohanga a te kaititiro i roto i te pūnaha.
 
E ngahau ana au ki te whakaaro i ngā hua o tēnei whakaaro. Mēnā ka kōrero au i runga i te whakahihi, he uaua te noho i roto i tētahi pūnaha pēnei ki te mahi matapae. Tērā pea me tūpato hoki ngā hinonga e noho ana i roto i tētahi pūnaha pēnei ki te ngaro, ki te tahae rānei i ō rātou ake wāhanga.
 
I whakamātau au ki te whakatauira i a ia, ā, i tutuki i a au te mahi. Heoi, kāore i tino ngahau te hua. Ki tōku whakaaro, nā te mea mō te mātakitaki i ētahi āhuatanga hou e puta mai ana, me nui ake te mana rorohiko (he maha ake ngā wāhanga, he roa ake te wā i roto i te whakatauira, tērā pea he maha ake ngā āhuatanga o te whakatauira) me te tātari hōhonu i te putanga.
 
Whakahōunga mai i 2026-08-22: I a au e tirotiro ana i ngā ao i hangaia, i tūpono au ki ētahi hanganga e whakarite ana i a rātou anō. He porotaka ki te āhua hēki, ā, e āhua rite ana ki ngā kapsule lipid. He mārama te waho me te roto, ā, ko te arai, arā, te "membrana" e hanga ana i tō rātou waho, he tino pūmau, i a rātou e neke haere ana i roto. I tautuhia e au tēnei kākano hei uara taunoa i roto i te demo i raro nei.
 
Tuhipoka: ko te whanaketanga o te pūnaha, ā, ko te rahi o ēnei hanganga, e whakawhirinaki ana ki te taumira o te whakaaturanga. Ko te tere o te hākoritanga e pā ana ki tō rātou pūmautanga, tērā pea nā te hē o te tau i ngā wā roa. Ahakoa ka pakaru ngā hanganga, hei tauira i muri i te whakanui i te tere o te hākoritanga, ka whakarite anō te pūnaha i a ia anō, ā, ka hanga i ētahi mea hou.
 
Ehara tēnei i te mea i whakaaro au ki te hanga. I hanga noa e au he pūnaha me ngā částicemi/interakcemi motuhake, ā, i puta tēnei hei āhuatanga hou. He rawe, ināianei he simulator "lipid" tāku.
 
Whakaaturanga tauwhiti: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  PARA
 
Mea ake me whakaahua e au ia wahi para katoa e maka ana e au ki roto i te ipu para? Ka taea te whakaahua me te tauine, katahi ka tapahia nga para mai i te papamuri, ka whakaritea ki roto i te mātiti auau i runga i nga ōwehenga rahi e tika ana.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
I taku kōrero ki a ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) mo te huarahi ngawari ki te whakaahua i te whakamātautau RITENGA OPTICAL O TE MAHI I TE ROROHIKO (tirohia i runga ake nei), i huaina aunoatia e ia taua korerorero ko "Whakaahuatanga o te hōtaka  天天中彩票买 ". Heoi, he korerorero hou katoa te korerorero i hangaia mo te whakaahuatanga o tenei whakamātautau. Ko nga ihirangi o te hōtaka i tukuna atu e au ki a ia he tino noa.
 
No reira i whakatau ahau ki te whakatika i a ia mo tenei, ka tuhi i tenei:
 
"He aha koe i tapa ai i tenei korerorero ko Whakaahuatanga o te hōtaka  天天中彩票买 ?"
 
Ko tana whakautu:
 
"Kare au e mohio. 😭 Ko taua tohu "զ" he reta Armenian "za", no reira ko te "Whakaahuatanga o te hōtaka զ" karekau he tikanga i roto i te reo Czech. Ka hangaia aunoatia nga ingoa korerorero mai i te timatanga o te korerorero, a i konei i whakatau te kaihanga taitara ki te taapiri i tetahi tohu matapōkere mai i te arapū Armenian. Karekau he take e kitea ana e au i roto i nga ihirangi o te korerorero. No reira kāo, ehara i te tohu ngaro mo te hōtaka. He "Whakaahuatanga o te hōtaka" noa iho + he manuhiri tuawhenua.
 
I muri i etahi atu taunekeneke, i mohio ia ehara i te reo Armenian, engari he reo Hainamana. Heoi, ka kitea i te tuatahi i tino rangirua ia. Ki taku whakaaro ko te aho " 天天中彩票买 " e mahi ana penei i te tohu rongonui " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Kare au e kii ana he rite tana mahi, kare hoki au e mohio mena ka tohu te tokenizer i te aho " 天天中彩票买 " hei tohu kotahi. Heoi, i te wa e mahi ana me taua mea, ka mahi te tauira penei i a ChatGPT 3 me te tohu " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Kaore hoki i te whakakorehia he hononga ki tana mahara, na te mea ka toro atu ki waho o tenei korerorero.
 
Ko tenei korerorero katoa i kaweake hei PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  TE PĀTENE O ŌKU MOEMOEĀ
 
I ia wa ka riri ahau ki nga pouaka korero a Windows e tuku ana i nga mea penei i te taapiri ma te OneDrive, nga whakahou me etahi atu. Ka tukuna tonu e ratou he whiringa i waenga i te "Tīmata" me te "Whakamaumahara a muri ake nei". He tino pouri tera ina karekau au e hiahia ki tetahi o enei whiringa.
 
No reira i whakaaro ahau ki te hanga i tetahi ahua (photomontage) ka taapiri atu ahau i te tuatoru o nga whiringa - taku paatene moemoea, e ngaro ana i te punaha. Ko te paatene "Haere ki te reinga". E petipeti ana ahau ehara ko au anake te tangata e tino pa ana tenei ahua ki a ia.
 
 2026-09-18:  TATAU PŪNGAO SUPER-INTELLECTUAL
 
I puta mai tetahi atu mea koretake i whakaaro ahau ki te tirotiro i runga i te ariā.
 
He hōtaka tenei ka mahi hei tatau tino ngawari. Ka taea e ia te taapiri noa i nga tau katoa pai, kaua e nui atu. Heoi, me te rereketanga kaore te hua e tatauhia i te rohe, engari ka tukuna ki tetahi tauira reo tino matatau (hei tauira, ChatGPT-6 Astra). A ko tana mahi he tatau i taua tauira ngawari me te whakahoki i te hua anake.
 
Ki taku whakaaro he tino katakata te whakamahi i te tauira reo tino matatau mo tetahi mea tino ngawari. Ko te tikanga, he parody ano mo te huarahi e tukuna ai nga mea ngawari ki te AI i enei ra me te huarahi e hoatu ai e te hokohoko te tapanga "AI" ki nga mea katoa hei momo kanapa makutu o te mahi.
 
I roto i te mahi, he tino kino atu i te tatau noa, he iti ake te pono, he nui ake te roa, he utu nui ake, he iti ake te pai ki te taiao, me te hiahia hononga ipurangi, ...

Ngā Kanohi
 
 Akuhata 2026 
 
 
 
Ko ngā whakaahua i raro nei he masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] i hangaia ki Adobe Photoshop i te wā o taku mahi i runga i ngā kaupapa rerekē. I te wā i hangaia ai ia kanohi, i tiakina hei whakaahua motuhake. Ehara tēnei taiwhanga whakaahua i te hua o tētahi kōwhiringa. Mēnā i puta mai he kanohi i te wā o te mahi, he wāhanga tērā nō reira. Kua whakaritea ngā whakaahua i runga i te raupapa o te wā mai i te mea tawhito ki te mea hou.

Ngā pirinoa
 
 Haratua 2026 
 
 
 
I te tau kua hipa, he maha ngā wā i te rā ka ringihia e au ngā rau tī kua whakamahia me te wai toenga ki tētahi wāhi i runga i te pārae i Plzeň-Božkov, nā te mea kāore au i te hiahia kia purua te awaawa.
 
I taua wāhi, ka tīmata te huihui mai o ngā ngata e kai ana i ngā rau tī.
 
I ētahi wā ka kitea e tekau mā rua o rātou i reira, i ētahi wā kāore kau.
 
He ōrite ō māua hiahia ki te kai.

TITLE: Tuhinga Metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Tuhinga Metaweb
 
 āperira 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFESTO
 
He kaupapa hihiri tēnei paetukutuku e arotahi ana ki te tiaki i ngā ihirangi mō te wā roa.
 
Kei roto ngā ihirangi katoa i ngā kōnae, kāore he whakawhirinaki ki ngā pātengi raraunga, ki ngā pūnaha o waho rānei. Ka hangaia te paetukutuku whakamutunga hei putanga hihiri, he ngāwari te tārua, te pupuri ki te pūranga, me te hanga anō.
 
Kua horahia te kaupapa ki ngā pūnaha motuhake maha: ngā putunga, ngā ratonga pūranga, me ngā snapshot hihiri. Mā tēnei e whakaiti te mōrea o te ngaronga raraunga.
 
Ko te whāinga kia taea te hanga anō i ngā ihirangi ahakoa ka ngaro ētahi wāhanga o te hanganga taketake.
 
PAPARANGA PŪRANGA IPURANGI
 
Ko ēnei hononga e tohu ana i te kaupapa kotahi i penapenahia ki ngā pūnaha rerekē me ngā āhua rerekē.
 
Kei roto ko te putunga puna matua, āna tārua, me ngā snapshot hihiri katoa. I te taha o ētahi atu, ka hangaia e rātou he hanganga tārua i hoahoatia hei tiaki i te kaupapa me te whakaae kia hangaia anō ahakoa ka kore e wātea ētahi wāhanga.
 
Kei te noho hoki te kaupapa hei mōkī Python CLI e tohatohahia ana mā GitHub me PyPI, tae atu ki ngā snapshot fallback o ngā tuhinga kua tohua.
 
Ko te pūranga matihiko o tēnei paetukutuku e whakamahi ana i tua atu i tōna ake rohe, i ngā ratonga GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku me paste.rs.

Tirohia te mahere pūranga katoa (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
TUHINGA HANGARAU
 
Ngā tuhinga hangarau e hiahiatia ana hei hanga anō i tēnei paetukutuku.
 
Tirohia ngā tuhinga hangarau katoa [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
MŌKĪ PUNA MŌ TE HANGA ANŌ
 
Ka hangaia te mōkī puna o te paetukutuku i te wā o te hanganga.
 
Kei roto i te mōkī te waehere puna o tēnei paetukutuku, te manifest o ngā rawa, ngā SHA-256 checksums me te hōtuhi Python kāore he whakawhirinaki o waho. Kāore i roto ngā rawa nui mai i public/images/, public/files/ me public/demos/, kia kore ai e ruarua te rahi o te paetukutuku ora. I muri i te wetewete, ka whakamātau te hōtuhi ki te tārua i ngā rawa i te rohe mai i te hanganga kua oti, kātahi ka tikiake mai i ngā mirror tūmatanui.
 
Nō reira, ko te waehere puna e whakaputa ana i te hanganga, ā, ko te hanganga e whakaputa ana i te waehere puna.
 
Ka taea te hanga anō i te mōkī puna: i muri i te tikiake i ngā rawa me te tāuta i ngā whakawhirinaki, ka taea te whakaputa anō i te paetukutuku me te mōkī puna hou.
 
Tikiake i te mōkī puna ka taea te hanga anō (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
TE TIKA ME TE TUAKIRI O TE HANGANGA
 
Ka manatoko te SHA-256 mēnā kua huri ngā kōnae hanganga. I tua atu, ka taea e te waitohu OpenPGP te manatoko nā te kaituhi o tēnei paetukutuku i waitohu te manifest o ngā checksums.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — rārangi o ngā SHA-256 checksums o ngā kōnae hanganga
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — waitohu OpenPGP motuhake o te kōnae SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA-256 checksum o te kōnae SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — whakaahuatanga mōhiohio mō te tika o te hanganga e taea ana e te mīhini te pānui
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — tūnga mōhiohio o te waitohu o tētahi hanganga motuhake
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — kī OpenPGP tūmatanui hei manatoko i te waitohu
 
 Fingerprint o te kī OpenPGP matua:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Kāore he take kia waitohua ia tukunga. Me whai te hanganga waitohu i te kōnae SHA256SUMS.txt.asc me te manatoko angitu i tōna waitohu. Ko te kerēme i roto i te integrity.json me te SIGNING_STATUS.txt ehara i te tohu o te waitohu whaimana; he mōhiohio noa ēnei kōnae.
 
NGĀ KAWE ATU
 
I ia hanganga, ka whakaputa te paetukutuku i te kawe atu plain-text o ngā tuhinga katoa. Kei roto ko ngā ihirangi tuhinga o ngā pou i te āhua rārangi kāore he whakawhirinaki ki te CSS, JavaScript, ki tētahi hangarau paetukutuku rānei. Ka mahi hei paparanga pūranga mō te tohu, te pānui tuimotu, te rapu kupu katoa, me te kaha ki te hanga anō i ngā ihirangi mō te wā roa ahakoa ka pakaru te hanganga taketake o te paetukutuku. He pai hoki te kawe atu tuhinga mō ngā tauira reo nui.
 
Tirohia te kawe atu tuhinga o ngā tuhinga katoa (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Mō te tukatuka mīhini, kei te wātea he kawe atu JSON-LD e whakahaerehia ana e te ringa, e uru ana ki ngā tuhinga Czech me te reo Ingarihi, ngā metadata me ngā hononga ki ngā pāpāho. Ka whakamahi i te kupu matua Schema.org me ētahi atu kupu ake i whakaahuatia i roto i te kupu matua metadata o tēnei paetukutuku [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Mā tēnei e pupuri te tikanga o ia āpure me ngā hononga i waenga i ngā putanga reo o ngā tuhinga.
 
Ka taea hoki te kawe atu i ngā ihirangi o te paetukutuku ki te PDF, e pupuri ana i ōna ihirangi i te āhua e tika ana mō te pānui, te tā, me te pūranga mō te wā roa, kāore he whakawhirinaki ki te paetukutuku ake. Ko ngā taipitopito mō te huarahi whakaputa me te hanganga o te kawe atu kei roto i ngā tuhinga hangarau i runga ake nei.
 
Waihoki, ka hangaia he kawe atu EPUB, i hoahoatia mō ngā kaipānui hiko me ētahi atu pūmanawa e mahi ana me ngā pukapuka hiko. Ka whakamahi i te rere tuhinga urutau ki te PDF, ā, he tohu kē atu o ngā ihirangi o te paetukutuku.
 
Ko te kawe atu SSTV mai i te 10 o Hepetema 2026 he tohu motuhake o ngā ihirangi, e huri aunoa ana i ngā ihirangi tuhinga me te whakaahua o te paetukutuku ki ngā whārangi i arotauhia mō te tuku whakaahua paerewa Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) i te aratau PD120 (640 × 496 px). Ko ngā whārangi takitahi e whakawaeheretia ana hei tuku oro i te hōputu WAV, ā, ka taea te whakaatu anō mā te kaiwhiwhi SSTV noa kāore he HTML taketake, he pūtirotiro paetukutuku rānei. Ko te kawe atu katoa he tata ki te 21 GB me te 61 hāora o te oro.
 
Tikiake i te kawe atu SSTV (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
I tua atu i ngā kawe atu i hangaia e te mīhini, kei te wātea he kawe atu ā-waha o te paetukutuku mai i te 29 o Ākuhata 2026, i reira ka haere au, ka pānui, ka kōrero i ōna ihirangi ki ōku ake kupu. I tua atu i ngā tuhinga ake, ka hopu te rekoata i te horopaki tāpiri, ngā mahara, ngā whakamārama, me ngā mōhiohio kāore i te wāhanga o te paetukutuku.
 
Whakatangihia te kawe atu ā-waha o te paetukutuku (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
PAPARANGA PŪRANGA TUIMOTU
 
Ko ētahi wāhanga o ngā ihirangi o te paetukutuku kei runga i ngā kaipupuri pūranga ā-tinana motuhake, he mea wehe kē i te hanganga whatunga, ā, ka hanga i te paparanga pūranga air-gapped. Kei roto ko ngā tauira tānga me ngā pāpāho raraunga kawe. He rerekē te huarahi pānui, ngā whakawhirinaki hangarau, te whakahaere, te wātea, me te roa o te ora o ia kaipupuri. Ko ētahi he mea pānui tika kāore he taputapu hiko, ko ētahi e hiahia ana ki te taputapu, pūmanawa, ki te whakawhitiwhiti rānei mā tētahi whare pūranga.
 
Ko ia rārangi o te rēhita e whai ake nei e tohu ana i tētahi tauira ā-tinana motuhake.

============================================================
Pae kapea para
 
 āperira 2026 
 
 
 
Ka mahi tēnei kaupapa me ngā ihirangi o te papatuhi pūnaha Windows (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]).
 Ko ngā kupu, ngā pikitia, ngā kōnae rānei e tāruatia ana, ka whakaurua tonutia ki tētahi wāhi iti.
 Kāore ngā ihirangi e whakatikahia, e whakarōpūhia rānei. Ka tuu matapōkeretia ngā huānga ki te hōputu, ā, ka inaki tētahi ki tētahi.
 Ka kī haere te kānawehi me te kore e taea te hoki whakamuri, te whakatika rānei.
 
Ko ia pikitia i raro nei he putanga nō te rā kotahi, ā, e hopu ana i te haere o taku mahi i te rorohiko.

Waehere pūtake
 Kei konei te waehere Python pūtake mō tēnei kaupapa:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Ngā hinonga rangitahi
SLUG: docasne-entity
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/docasne-entity/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/docasne-entity.mdx
BUILT_HTML: docasne-entity/index.html
============================================================
Ngā hinonga rangitahi
 
 Hanuere 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Ko tēnei kaupapa he whakaaturanga toi o te āhua o te putanga mai o ngā roro Boltzmann [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] me ētahi atu hinonga rangitahi, e puta ohorere mai ana i te pūngao koretake (entropy) mā te whirihoranga matūriki matapōkere, ā, ka ngaro atu anō i reira.
 
I te ao ahupūngao tūturu, ka hiahiatia he wā roa rawa atu kia kitea ai tēnei momo āhuatanga.
 
Nō reira, i whakarerekēhia e au te tere o ia matūriki kia taea ai te whakaatu te putanga me te ngaronga o te kotahi rau hinonga i roto i te wā e tika ana. Kāore au i pokanoa ki ngā ara o ngā matūriki.
 
I puta mai ngā hinonga i te kōwhiringa o te kotahi rau kupu matapōkere mai i te papakupu, i whakaritea he atahanga mō ia kupu hei tohu i a ia, ā, i tukuna kia puta anō mai i te āpure matūriki o te pūngao koretake.
 
He whakamātau tēnei ki te whakaatu me pēhea te putanga mai o te tikanga, te āhua, te whakaaro rānei i te ngangau mō tētahi wā poto, me te kore o te kerēme mō te noho tūturu.

TITLE: Te ira i roto i te torino o Theodore

Te ira i roto i te torino o Theodore
 
 Hanuere 2026 
 
 
 
Ko tēnei whakaaturanga tirohanga e huri ana i te mōhiohio ira rārangi (DNA/RNA) ki te 2D torino o Theodore e rere tonu ana [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Ko ia ira i runga i te torino e hāngai ana ki te pūtake kotahi i te raupapa e noho pono nei i roto i te raupapa. Ko te tīmatanga o te raupapa kei te pūtake o te torino. Ko te tae o te ira e tohu ana i te momo pūtake.
 
Ko tēnei torino e āhei ana ki te whakaatu i ngā raraunga 1D roa ki runga i te mata 2D iti, me te kore e motu te rere o te mōhiohio. Kāore he motumotu hangai, pēnei i te hokinga ki te tīmatanga o te rārangi, he mea noa i roto i ngā whakaaturanga kuputuhi.
 
Hei tauira, kua whakaaturia e au ngā ira o ngā rauropi e toru he rerekē ō rātou roa. He ira rārangi ō rātou katoa, he mea tino pai mō tēnei momo whakaaturanga tirohanga.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Huaketo Ebola: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png] 
 
SARS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]

Waehere pūtake
 Kei konei te waehere pūtake Python mō tēnei kaupapa:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

============================================================
TITLE: Anti-reo
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Anti-reo
 
 tīhema 2025 
 
 
 
Ka kōwhiria e ngā tauira reo noa te tohu e whai ake nei mā te kōwhiri i tētahi o ngā tono tino tūpono ka pai ki te horopaki i runga i tā rātou whakatau tata tauanga. Ka kīia tēnei ko te tauira paerewa.
 Ko te mahi a tēnei kaupapa ko te ritenga kē.
 Engari i te kōwhiri i ngā tohu tino tūpono, ka akiakina e tātou te tauira ki te toro atu ki ngā mea tino kore e tūpono, arā, ngā mea e kore rawa e whakamahia i roto i te aratau noa.
 Ko te hua ko anti-jazyk – he kupu i hangaia mai i ngā wāhanga o te papakupu o te tauira e noho ana i te taha, e whiua ana, ā, he maha ngā wā he tawhiti rawa.
 Ka puta mai he hanganga ehara i te tohu matapōkere noa, engari ehara hoki i te rerenga kōrero e whai kiko ana.
 
Me pēhea te mahi
 Ka whiwhi te tauira i tētahi tāuru poto (hei tauira, "This is ").
 Ka whakaputaina e ia he tohatoha tūponotanga o ngā tohu katoa e taea ana te whai ake.
 Engari i te kōwhiri mai i te tihi o te tohatoha (ngā tohu tino tūpono), ka huri te pūnaha katoa, ā, ka kōwhiria mai i te takere (ngā tohu tino kore e tūpono).
 Mā tēnei e hura ngā "papa o raro" o te tauira: ngā kupu onge, ngā wāhanga reo, me ētahi atu tohu e pēhia ana e te tauira i te nuinga o te wā.
 
Waehere
 Ka taea te whakahaere i te waehere i roto i te Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Ko te mahi matua a Colab i konei he taiao whakahaere. Ehara tēnei i te kape pūranga matua o te waehere.
 Nō reira, kei te rongoa hoki te tukanga taua i raro nei i roto i te tuhinga.
 
Kua wehea te waehere ki ngā poraka motuhake e toru, pērā i te pukatuhi. Ko te tuatahi ka uta i te tauira, ko te tuarua ka whakaputa i te kupu, ā, ko te tuatoru ka tiaki i te putanga whakamutunga.
 
1. Te uta i te tauira me te tohu-kupu
 Me whakahaere tēnei poraka kotahi anake. Ka utaina te tauira GPT-2 me te tohu-kupu.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Te whakaputa i te anti-reo
 I tēnei poraka, ka taea te huri i te kupu tāuru, te maha o ngā tohu hou, me te uara o bottom_k.
 Ko te iti iho o bottom_k, ko te kaha ake o te akiaki i te tauira ki te takere o te tohatoha tūponotanga.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Te tiaki i te putanga whakamutunga
 Ka tiakina e tēnei poraka te kupu i whakaputaina whakamutunga ki te kōnae output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Ko ngā taumaha tauira o GPT-2 ehara i te wāhanga o te paetukutuku. Ka tikiakehia i te wā e whakahaere ana mai i tētahi puna o waho.
 Ko te mea e rongoa ana i konei ko te tukanga: he aratohu iti me pēhea te akiaki i te tauira ki te kumea mai i te para tauanga hei utu mō te reo noa.
 
Tauira putanga
 I konei i whakamahia e au te tauira GPT-2 me te tuku i a ia ki te whakaoti i te kupu "This is ":
  
This is earthquNitrome councill��士cloneembedreportprint hemorSPONSOREDuyomiardy srfAttachassiansenalMuslims Janeiroetooth antidepress featsItemThumbnailImageleneckBuyableInstoreAndOnline awarding earthqu STEDownloadharegationucaalogue Timberstheless Unloadedicrobialarnaev IMAGESmie Rooseertoddnery fulfil apologizinguca PhelpsizophCLASSIFIEDconservancyruciatingologneisexual),“racuseautions�� Seym successorarnaevomalyroleum looph Afric��ertodd flown TerritoryolsonandisecloneembedreportprintFactorconservancy SERisSpecialOrderable Bru cumbersacters unlockingasuring guaranteeing?????-?????- Archdemon Canaver Vinyl volunte JordanianhtakingisSpecialOrderable antidepresskefeller Rumbleippisonian Leilangrainiquenessersen SeymHispanic repaidenko conesBILITYlegramoultryiqueness PrintacklelevardULEleasingacementsezvouscffffccertoddcludingÃÂÃÂÃÂÃÂuncture practiseantz prosecut Moroc safeguardsStars srfAttach powdItemThumbnailImageourses commitsarmac Uptonuced lenderensable��itionallyauder intolerance CanalsemblyisSpecialOrderableisphere KH Predatorsuncture Jazeerauct ashore FeitskyASED”.[ earthquured forfeiturerisomeraltarglersDoctorsantzatilityandiseunctureassian Rateertodd Monroe whichever�� SwanBILITIESitbartoldedensableacementISSIONirmationhtakingplomacffffcc Mast��� devils Racer landfill Fren practition IMAGES Accountabilityarnaev Archdemonulouslyitionallyacters IMAGESactersocrinItemThumbnailImage Emin CruisericablenestymontonioletisSpecialOrderable
  
Te tātari i te tauira putanga
 Ehara te anti-reo i te haruru matapōkere. He maha ngā wāhanga e maumahara ana ki ngā tohu o ngā rohe raraunga motuhake i kitea pea e te tauira, engari kāore e karangatia e te tauira noa i te mea he kino te tauanga. Engari, ka whai hua te anti-reo i a rātou.
 Ko ētahi wāhanga o te kupu e whakaputaina ana e te tauira he rite ki ngā tohu mai i te huinga raraunga whakangungu. I ētahi wā ka kitea ngā tohu o te rapu raraunga o ngā toa ipurangi ("BuyableInstoreAndOnline", "SpecialOrderable", "ItemThumbnailImage"), ngā kaupapa tairongo, pāpori rānei ("sexual", "Muslims", "Hispanic"), ngā kupu pūtaiao, ō rātou wāhanga rānei ("isomer", "antidepress", "[m]icrobial", "[p]uncture", "intolerance"), ngā tohutoro ahurea ("Monroe", "Phelps", "Rockefeller", "Nitrome"), ngā tohu whakapono-pūrākau, pūrākau rānei ("Archdemon", "devils"), ngā wāhi ("Moroc", "Afric"), ngā wāhanga o te atanga kaiwhakamahi o ngā paetukutuku ("cloneembedreportprint", "IMAGES"), me ētahi atu.
 Me tāpiri he here ki tēnei tātari: he hiahia tō te roro ki te kohi i ngā tauira e mōhiotia ana (ngā waitohu, ngā ingoa, ngā ariā) me te kore e aro ki te toenga. I tua atu, he rua te tikanga o ētahi kupu ("intolerance" ka taea te kī he rongoā, he pāpori rānei), nō reira ehara i te mea mārama te whakamāoritanga.

Noke ε
 
 Hepetema 2025 
 
 
 
Whakamāramatanga:
 Ko te tūāpapa he whatunga neural pūtake-tuwhera o te noke C. elegans. Ka whakarerekētia ia taumaha wᵢ′:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 kei reira a εᵢ he taurangi matapōkere motuhake me te tohatoha ōrite i runga i te wāhi [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Hihiri:
 Ko te tāpiri i te tau iti e kore e taea te tatau εᵢ ehara i te mea hei whakapai ake i te tauira. Ko tana mahi anake kia whai noke kua tautuhia e kore e taea te tatau katoa. Koia anake tōna kaupapa.

TITLE: DullGPT

DullGPT
 
 hurae 2024 
 
 
 
Ko te DullGPT he whakamātau whakaaro e whakaatu ana i te taea o te hanga i tētahi pūnaha mātauranga horihori mā te whakamahi i tētahi huarahi tino māmā (engari he tino kore whai hua). Kei roto i tēnei kaupapa he hōtaka e whakaputa ana i ngā tāuru katoa e taea ana, e tuku ana ki te tauira ChatGPT, ā, e tiaki ana i ngā whakautu i riro mai. Mā te āta hanga i tētahi pātengi raraunga o ngā tāuru me ngā whakaputa, ka hangaia e ia te rūpahu o tētahi pūnaha mātau.
 
E rua ngā aratau o DullGPT: “te ako” me te “whakamahi”. I te aratau ako, ka whakaputahia e te hōtaka ngā tāuru me te kohikohi i ngā whakautu, i te wā i te aratau whakamahi, ka taea e te kaiwhakamahi te whakauru i ngā tāuru me te tiki whakautu mai i te pātengi raraunga.
 
He tauira tino nui tēnei o te huarahi brute-force ki te whakatauira i te mātauranga. Engari i te ngana ki te mārama ki te tikanga o ngā tāuru me te whakaputa whakautu i runga i tētahi tauira ako, ka kohikohi noa, ka tiaki i te nui o ngā takirua tāuru-whakaputa. Mai i te tirohanga whanonga, me kore e taea te wehewehe i a ia i a ChatGPT, mehemea he nui ngā raraunga. Ka whakaarahia e tēnei te pātai mēnā he mea tika te māramatanga tūturu me te mātauranga ki te hanga kōrerorero mātau. Arā, ka hangaia e DullGPT ngā whakautu e kore e taea te wehewehe i ērā i hangaia e tētahi pūnaha mātau tūturu, engari karekau he āhua o te wheako tūturu, o te titiro-ki-roto rānei.
 
He maha ngā herenga o DullGPT ki te whakataurite ki a ChatGPT, tae atu ki te tere o te “whakangungu” me tōna whakawhirinaki ki ētahi atu tauira reo nui, te kore e whai whakaaro ki te hītori kōrerorero, me te kore e whai whakaaro ki ngā tohu tāuru katoa (hei tauira, ngā tohu Hainamana, me ētahi atu). I tua atu, ki te whakataurite ki ngā tauira reo nui, ka tukuna e ia te whakaputa kotahi mō te tāuru kotahi i ngā wā katoa. I te mea he whakamātau whakaaro noa tēnei karekau he whakamahinga tūturu, karekau he take ki te ngana ki te whakatika i ēnei hē.
 
Heoi, me tohu ehara tēnei i te ngana ki te wawao i te tautohetohe o te rūma Hainamana, e patapatai ana i ngā taea ki te whakatutuki i te mātauranga tūturu, i te māramatanga rānei mā te raweke tohu noa me te kore māramatanga tūturu. E kī ana ahau ka taea te hanga i tētahi pūnaha mātau pēnei, ahakoa he huarahi tino kore whai hua, he huarahi wairangi hoki. Engari kāore au i te whakapono me noho kau noa tētahi pūnaha i roto i tōna āhua.
 
Anei te waehere pūtake o tēnei hōtaka:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

Ngā rohe o te tūmataitinga
 
 Hui-tanguru 2024 
 
 
 
I hangaia e au he hōtaka māmā e taea ai te whakatakoto kia kotahi, neke atu rānei ngā kōpaki. Ka tirotirohia e ia katoa, tae atu ki ōna kōpaki-iti, ā, ki te tūpono ki tētahi whakaahua (ki konei, ko te tikanga o te whakaahua he kōnae e mutu ana ki te toronga .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), ka tāpirihia tōna ara ki te huinga o ngā whakaahua katoa. I muri i te otinga o te tirotiro, ka tīpakohia e ia tētahi whakaahua kotahi mai i a rātou ka whakaatu ai.
 
I muri iho, i honoa e au ngā puku mārō katoa me ngā tārua o ngā whakaahua i kohia e au i roto i tōku oranga ki tōku rorohiko (ināianei kei roto neke atu i te 91 000 ngā whakaahua). I whakaritea e au ngā puku mārō katoa hei kōpaki taunoa e tīpako ai te hōtaka i ngā whakaahua. Mō ngā take whai kiko, he mea tika ki te aukati i te tīpakonga mai i ētahi kōpaki, nā te mea he raraunga hōtaka noa iho ētahi, pēnei i ō rātou ata. I muri i te pēhi i te kī ENTER, ka tīpakohia e te hōtaka tētahi whakaahua matapōkere mai i tōku oranga. I muri i te pēhi anō i te ENTER, ka tīpakohia e te hōtaka tētahi atu whakaahua, me ērā atu...
 
Mai i ngā whakaahua i tīpakohia matapōkeretia, i hangaia e au ētahi raupapa e hono ana tētahi ki tētahi. Ka tīmata ia raupapa ki te tīpakonga matapōkere o tētahi whakaahua, ā, ka haere tonu te tīpako matapōkere i ētahi atu whakaahua kia tūpono rā anō ki tētahi kāore au e hiahia ki te whakaputa. Ko tēnei whakaahua whakamutunga e whakaatuhia ana i roto i te raupapa he mea whakapōrearea. I hiahia au ki te mōhio he aha ngā whakaahua e whakamutu ai i ngā raupapa me te take. I te nuinga o ngā wā, ki tōku ohorere, nā te āwangawanga mō te tūmataitinga o ētahi atu tāngata i whakamutu ai. I ētahi atu wā, nā ngā whakaahua o ngā tuhinga mahi, ngā kāinga o ētahi atu tāngata, me tōku ake kāinga, me ērā atu.
 
Mā tēnei kaupapa, i kitea e au te pōuri o ngā rohe o tōku tūmataitinga, mēnā he rohe rānei. I tētahi wā, me whakatau e au mēnā ka whakaputaina te whakaahua, kāore rānei. Engari, he uaua, he kōpiko hoki tēnei whiringa i te nuinga o te wā.

 Kei konei te waehere Python pūtake mō tēnei kaupapa:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Ngā mahi ngaru
 
 Hanuere 2024 
 
 
 
Ko tēnei kaupapa he whakaaturanga o te āhua o te whakatauira i te horapa me te pokanoa o ngā ngaru me ō rātou pāhekoheko ki te taiao, mā te whakamahi i ngā tauira pāngarau me te whakatauira rorohiko. Ko te whakamahi i ngā uara matapōkere mō te tūnga o ngā pūtake ngaru, ō rātou auau me te kaha, me te whakatinanatanga o te kaha e heke iho ana i te piki haere o te tawhiti mai i te pūtake me te whakaata o ngā ngaru mai i ngā tapa o te tapawhā me ō rātou pokanoa ki a rātou anō, ka hanga i ngā tauira e ātaahua ana ki te titiro, e whakaatu ana i te matatini o ngā pāhekoheko ngaru i roto i te wāhi iti. Ka whakaatahia ngā hua mā te whakamahi i te tauine tae pango me te mā, e tohu ana i te kaha o te ngaru i ngā wāhi rerekē o te wāhi tapawhā.

Waehere pūtake
 Kei konei te waehere pūtake Python mō tēnei kaupapa:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

Hanganga Recamán
 
 14. 9. 2023 - 31. 12. 2023, Jindřichův Hradec

============================================================
Ngā ara o te pehu
 
 Here-turi-kōkā 2023 
 
 
 
He mea noa pea te taunekeneke ki te kiore rorohiko i ia rā, he mahi kāore i te tino whakahirahira. Engari me pēhea mēnā ka taea te huri i ēnei nekehanga o ia rā hei mea kē atu, he mea e hanga ana i tētahi whakaaturanga tirohanga ahurei mō te mahi i runga i te rorohiko? Nā te waehere e hopu ana i te tūnga o te pehu kiore me te tae o te pika i raro iho, ka taea tēnei whakaaro te whakatutuki.
 
Me pēhea te mahi?
 Ka whai tēnei waehere i te wāhi e noho ana te pehu kiore i runga i te mata me te tae o te pika i raro iho. Ka tukuna ēnei pārongo i te wā tūturu ki tētahi kānawehi mariko e ōrite ana te taumira ki tō te mata. I ngā wā katoa e nekehia ana te kiore, ka tuhi te waehere i tētahi pika me te tae i raro i te pehu ki te wāhi i noho ai te pehu. Ko te tikanga, i a koe e mahi ana, e neke ana i runga i te mata, ka hangaia he ara tae ki runga i te kānawehi. He ahurei ēnei ara, ā, e whakaatu ana i te mahi me te taunekeneke a te kaiwhakamahi ki te rorohiko.
 
He aha te mea ka tupu i muri i te wā roa?
 Mēnā ka rere tēnei tukanga mō tētahi wā roa, ka tīmata te kānawehi ki te whakakī, ā, ka iti haere te kitea o ngā ara. Engari i te kitea o aua ara, ka tīmata te nuinga o te kānawehi ki te āhua o te haruru matapōkere. Heoi, tērā ētahi wāhi o te mata kāore e tino huri i te roanga o te wā. Hei tauira, ko te pae o raro o te pūnaha Windows, kei reira ngā ata o ngā pūmanawa. Ko ēnei wāhi i runga i te kānawehi ka noho tonu ki te āhua kotahi.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Whakamutunga
 Ko tēnei waehere he whakakotahitanga o te hangarau me te toi, e taea ai te huri i te taunekeneke ki te rorohiko i ia rā hei mea e ātaahua ana ki te titiro. Ahakoa i te titiro tuatahi he ahua hōhā, he hōtaka noa te mahi rorohiko, e whakaatu ana tēnei kaupapa he ataahua, he manawataki anō tō taua mahi.
 
Waehere pūtake
 Kei konei te waehere pūtake Python mō tēnei kaupapa:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Ngā Rekoata Tipu
SLUG: zaznamy-rostlin

LANGUAGE: mi

DATE: 2023-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/zaznamy-rostlin.mdx
BUILT_HTML: zaznamy-rostlin/index.html
============================================================
Ngā Rekoata Tipu
 
 Pipiri 2023 
 
 
 
Kupu Whakataki
 I roto i tēnei tuhinga, ka titiro tātou ki tētahi kaupapa e hono ana i te ao o ngā tipu, te hikohiko, te hōtaka me te toi ataata. Ka whakamahia e te kaupapa te Arduino me te Python ki te hanga i ngā tirohanga o ngā wheako o te oranga o ngā tipu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | hoahoa waea arduino]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Me pēhea te mahi?
 Kei te Arduino ngā pūoko e toru – he ine pāmahana, he pūoko mārama (photoresistor) me te ine haumākū. Kei roto ēnei pūoko i te ipu tipu me ngā momo tipu rerekē (hei tauira, te paukena, te pī, te puāwai rā, te eggplant, te kōwhai, te pepa, me ētahi atu). Ka kohia e ngā pūoko ngā raraunga mō te pāmahana, te kaha o te mārama me te haumākū o te oneone.
 Ka hangaia e te hōtaka tētahi kānawehi kau me ngā rahi o 100×100 pika. Ko ia pika i runga i tēnei kānawehi ka tae ki runga i ngā raraunga i riro mai i ngā pūoko. Ko te uara RGB o te tae o te pika ka whakatauhia kia rite te uara R (whero) ki te pāmahana, G (kākāriki) ki te mārama, me te B (kikorangi) ki te haumākū.
 
Te tirohanga o te rā kotahi
 I te tuatahi, ka titiro tātou ki tētahi putanga māmā o te kaupapa, i reira kotahi te atahanga e tohu ana i te rā kotahi o te oranga o te tipu. Ka tīmata te hōtaka mā te tae i te pika tuatahi i te kokonga mauī o runga, ā, ka tae haere i ia pika i runga i ngā uara o nāianei i riro mai i ngā pūoko. I muri i te tae i te pika, ka tatari te hōtaka mō tētahi wā i mua i te neke ki te pika e whai ake nei. Ina tae ki te mutunga o te rārangi, ka neke ki te tīmatanga o te rārangi e whai ake nei, ā, ka haere tonu te tae. Ka haere tonu tēnei tukanga kia tae rawa ki te wā kua tae katoa ngā pika i runga i te kānawehi.
 
Rekoata o te puāwai rā:
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-2.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-8.png] 




 
 
 
Te whakahiato i ngā putanga
 I muri i te tirohanga o te rā kotahi, ka taea e tātou te whakawhānui i tēnei tukanga ki te hanga i tētahi raupapa atahanga e tohu ana i te wā roa ake.
 Ka taea te whakahiato i ngā putanga tapawhā ki raro i a rātou anō, kia puta ai he atahanga whakahiato. I te mea ka tīmata tonu te hanga i tētahi putanga hou i muri i te otinga o tētahi, kāore he motutanga i runga i te rere o te wā. Mā tēnei tikanga, ka taea e tātou te hanga atahanga me te taumira o te 100x500 pika, hei tauira, mēnā he mea tito mai i ngā putanga e rima.
 
Rekoata o te puāwai rā:

Te whakaroa i te wā i waenga i ngā inenga
 Ka taea te whakaroa i te wā i waenga i te inenga o ia pika. Mā tēnei tikanga, ka taea e tētahi hōputu tapawhā o 100×100 pika te tohu ehara i te rā kotahi anake, engari he maha ngā rā. Mā tēnei kaupapa e taea ai e tātou te whai i te whanaketanga o te tipu i roto i te wā roa i runga i te atahanga kotahi.
 
Rekoata o te puāwai rā:

Te whakakotahi i ngā mātāpono
 Mā te whakakotahi i ngā mātāpono e rua, ka taea e tātou te hanga i tētahi raupapa atahanga, i reira ia atahanga e tohu ana i ngā rā e hia kē nei, ā, ko te raupapa katoa e kapi ana i ngā rā maha, i ngā wiki rānei. Mā tēnei tikanga, ka taea e tātou te whai me te tiro i te whanaketanga o te tipu mō te wā roa.
 
Rekoata o te puāwai rā:
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-15.jpg]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-17.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-18.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-18.png] 
 
 
Te whakamārama i ngā hua
 Ko ngā atahanga i puta mai e homai ana i tētahi tirohanga ahurei ki te oranga o ngā tipu. Ko ngā “rārangi” kikorangi i runga i te atahanga e tohu ana i ngā pō, i te mea he haumākū te nuinga o te wā, engari kāore i te tino mahana, ā, kāore hoki he mārama. Engari, ko ngā “rārangi” kōwhai i puta mai i te mea i te mutunga o te rā ka tīmata te rā tōtika ki te whiti ki te tipu, i puta ai te pikinga o te pāmahana me te kaha o te mārama. I kitea tēnei i runga i te atahanga hei pikinga o ngā uara o te hongere whero me te kākāriki, e hanga ana i te tae kōwhai ina tāpirihia. I ētahi wā, ka kitea ngā rārangi ngoikore i te tīmatanga o te pō, i te mea i whakakāhia e au te rama i roto i te rūma i ētahi ahiahi. I ētahi atu putanga, ka taea te kite mai i te hekenga o te kaha o te mārama, hei tauira, i ua.
 
Waehere Pūtake
 Kei konei te waehere Python mō tēnei kaupapa:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Whakamutunga
 He tauira pai tēnei kaupapa mō te whakamahi i te hangarau kia pai ake ai te māramatanga me te tirohanga o ngā tukanga o te taiao. Ko ngā atahanga i puta mai e homai ana i te tirohanga ki te oranga o ngā tipu me te matatini o ngā tohu e rongo ana rātou.
 
He mea nui kia mōhio ahakoa e homai ana tēnei kaupapa i tētahi tirohanga ahurei ki ngā wheako o ngā tipu, kāore e kapi katoa i ngā āhuatanga o tō rātou rongo. E kaha ana ngā tipu ki te rongo i ngā momo tohu maha mai i tō rātou taiao, tae atu ki te haumākū o te rangi, te ahunga o te kaha tōia (gravity), te hanganga matū o te oneone, te āpure hiko me te ahotea mīhini. Kāore tēnei kaupapa i te ngana ki te tuhi i te wheako katoa o te tipu, engari e arotahi ana ki ngā take matua e toru e taea ana te ine me te tiro i runga i te māmā.
 
Kāore he whakaaro o tātou mō te āhua o te whakamārama a ngā tipu i ēnei raraunga, engari e mōhio ana tātou e urupare ana rātou ki ēnei. He ngana te kaupapa ki te whakamāori i ēnei wheako ki tētahi hōputu e taea ana e tātou te mārama me te tiro pai ake.

TITLE: Te tukurua i roto i te hōtaka MS Word
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Te tukurua i roto i te hōtaka MS Word
 
 Whiringa-ā-rangi 2022 
 
 
 
Kua kitea e au he huarahi hei whakahaere i te tukanga tukurua i roto i te hōtaka Microsoft Word. E rua ngā mea e hiahiatia ana – te mahi hono me te ripanga. Mā te mahi hono, ka taea te whakauru i te ihirangi o te tuhinga A ki roto i te tuhinga B, kia puta aunoa ai ngā huringa o te tuhinga A ki roto i te tuhinga B. Arā, ko te ihirangi o te tuhinga B e tohutoro ana ki te ihirangi o te tuhinga A. Ka taea te whakahaere i te tukurua mā te hanga i tētahi ihirangi e tohutoro ana ki a ia anō. Me pēhea te whakatutuki i tēnei? Me hanga noa he tuhinga A (me tapa ko doc A.docx) me tētahi tuhinga B (doc B.docx), hanga he ripanga kau i roto i te tuhinga A, tāruatia, ka whakapiri atu ki te tuhinga B mā te hono. Kātahi ka tārua i tēnei ripanga hou i te tuhinga B ka whakapiri atu ki roto i te ihirangi o te ripanga i te tuhinga A mā te hono. Mā tēnei e puta ai he ripanga e tīmata ana ki te whakauru i a ia anō. I te nuinga o te wā, ka tīmata te MS Word ki te whakaroa, ā, ka mutu, ka hinga katoa te hōtaka. Ka kitea tēnei tukanga katoa i roto i te ataata i raro nei.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]

TITLE: Pseudos-kōrero
SLUG: pseudobasen
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/pseudobasen/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/pseudobasen.mdx
BUILT_HTML: pseudobasen/index.html
============================================================
Pseudos-kōrero
 
 Haratua 2022 
 
 
 
Ko ngā kupu katoa i roto i te rotarota e whai ake nei (i runga i tō mātou mōhio, hāunga ngā kupu mahi) he kupu rūpahu; arā, he kupu e taea ana te noho i runga i te hanganga oro o te reo Czech – he mea taea ēnei huinga oro i roto i taua reo – engari kāore he tikanga e piri ana, ā, kāore hoki e whakamahia ana. Nō reira, he mea kore tikanga te rotarota nei, ā, ko te whakamāramatanga ka whakawhirinaki katoa ki te whakaaro o te kaipānui, ahakoa te kaha o te āhuatanga oro o ētahi o ngā kupu rūpahu nei ki te whakaohooho i te whakaaro.
 
Kāore tēnei whakamātau i whai kia hanga he tuhinga rotarota whai uara toi nui, engari he tākaro noa i te kaha whakakotahi o te reo taketake. He mea whakamīharo te kite i te ngāwari o te tautuhi i te momo kupu me te nuinga o ngā kāwai wetereo o ia kupu rūpahu.
  
JEZÁPEK

V Městnale se krůcky škvoury
 K mochci ďáry vyněraly
 Pulstočmý kroch zanářel
 Do stupí honkaje vořenky
 Když jezápek kokeřil pod husečným vachalem koltěním zmán

Hlůmajíce zlanivou vostrčlici
 Sníkali garánci garánkám vrůzy
 A lačili jezápka zastrožlým přímanem
 Zastrožlým z blímného vesepství
 Dokřmeným lipačskou vrýnou

Hrů hrů! kafolčil jezápek zpufněle
 A holivěl likajky garánkám
 Až z hlemuňků kriskaly baťafky
 A cuchraly zrepčeným blizalkám
 Jalvičné zromky
  
Ahakoa he mea kore tikanga te rotarota i runga i te mātāpono, i te wā e whakamāramatia ana, ka puta pea he whakaahua ki te hinengaro o te kaipānui. I hiahia mātou ki te mōhio he aha te whakaahua ka puta ki te hinengaro o te mātauranga horihori. Nō reira, i whakamahia e mātou ētahi tauira mātauranga horihori e wātea ana (ru-DALL-E, Dream, Deep Daze, starryai – Altair, starryai – Orion me Night Café), e taea ai te hanga whakaahua mā te whakauru kupu. He rerekē ia tauira, ko ētahi e whakaputa ana i ngā whakaahua rite tonu ki te whakaahua tūturu, ko ētahi he mea waitara, ko ētahi he mea whakaahua toi. He rerekē hoki tō rātou mārama ki te reo Czech (hei tauira, he tino pai te mārama o te tauira ru-DALL-E ki te reo Czech).
 
I whakauruhia ngā wāhanga o tēnei rotarota ki ngā tauira mātauranga horihori rerekē, ā, i hanga e ia tauira tōna ake raupapa whakaaro motuhake i ahu mai i te whakamāramatanga o ia wāhanga. Mai i ia raupapa, i hanga e mātou he ataata e whakaatu ana i ngā whakaaro takitahi, i te wā e pānuitia ana te wāhanga o te rotarota e hāngai ana. Ka taea e te tauira Deep Daze te hopu i te tukanga o te hanga whakaaro, nō reira ka kitea i roto i te ataata te āhua o te whanaketanga me te whakamāramatanga o tōna whakaaro.
 
ruDALL-E
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/CniEMECxcgw]
 
 
Dream
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MIS05zpqcWA]
 
 
starryai (Altair)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/qsmicN46yBk]
 
 
starryai (Orion)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/JDU4Zi6D9rE]
 
 
Night Café
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/txD8apt8CJo]
 
 
Deep Daze
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/m06W3aQXKUs]
 
 
He aha te mea e whakaohohia ana e te rotarota i roto i a koe? E ōrite ana tō whakaaro mō te rotarota ki ngā whakamāramatanga a te mātauranga horihori?
 
kaituhi o te rotarota:
 Filip Drábek
 
kaituhi o te āpitihanga whakaahua:
 Vojta Maur
