TITLE: Te kōiri o te kupu Fibonacci
SLUG: spirala-fibonacciho-slova
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/spirala-fibonacciho-slova/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-08-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/spirala-fibonacciho-slova.mdx
BUILT_HTML: spirala-fibonacciho-slova/index.html
============================================================
Te kōiri o te kupu Fibonacci
 
 Here-turi-kōkā 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg | Te kōiri o te kupu Fibonacci: he putanga o te hōtaka a Pavel Burda]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Hei tuhi i tēnei āhua, i whakaurua e au te kupu Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_slovo] ki roto i te kōiri i runga i te tikanga ōrite ki te whakaurunga o ngā tau māori ki roto i te kōiri o Ulam. I tohua e au ngā tau takitahi i roto i tēnei kōiri i runga i te ture: 0 = tapawhā pango, 1 = tapawhā mā (e whakaaturia ana tēnei tukanga i te hākoritanga i raro nei). Hei whakatika i tēnei tukanga, i whakamahia te reo hōtaka JavaScript.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif | he tauira hākoritanga o te tukanga]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif] 
 
He aha te kupu Fibonacci?
 Ko te kupu Fibonacci he raupapa motuhake o ngā mati rua (he tohu rānei e rua i te nuinga o te wā). Ka riro mai i a tātou i runga i te tikanga ōrite ki te raupapa Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_posloupnost], engari hei utu mō te tāpiri, ka whakamahia e tātou te honohono o ngā kupu. E rua ngā wāhanga tuatahi kua tautuhia: S0 = 0, S1 = 01. Ko te wāhanga e whai ake nei i hangaia mā te honohono (arā, mā te tāpiri i te wāhanga i mua i te wāhanga whakamutunga ki muri i te wāhanga whakamutunga) ko S2 = 010. Nō reira, ko te raupapa o ngā mati tuatahi o ngā kupu Fibonacci ka pēnei te āhua: 0100101001001010010100100101001001…
 
He aha te kōiri o Ulam?
 Ko te kōiri o Ulam [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ulamova_spir%C3%A1la], arā, te kōiri tau pirimia, he pikitia ka puta mai i te whakaraupapa i ngā tau māori ki roto i te kōiri me te whakanui i ngā tau pirimia. I kitea e te tohunga pāngarau a Stanisław Ulam i te tau 1963. I tuhia e Ulam ngā tau māori ki roto i te mātiti tapawhā, te tau kotahi ki waenganui, me ētahi atu tau e kōiri ana ki waho.
 
Waehere pūtake o te kaupapa
 
[CODE BLOCK]
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Spirála Fibonacciho slova</title>

  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">

  <style>
    #container {
      text-align: center;
      position: absolute;
      left: 50%;
      top: 50%;
      padding: 20px;
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      width: 600px;
      height: 600px;
      background-color: white;
    }

    #point {
      color: black;
      background-color: white;
      border:none;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }

    #point2 {
      color: white;
      background-color: black;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      border:none;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main id='container'>
  </main>

  <script>
    /* FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    function spiral(n) {
      if (n === 0) return [0, 0, r];
      --n;

      var r = Math.floor((Math.sqrt(n + 1) - 1) / 2) + 1;
      var p = (8 * r * (r - 1)) / 2;
      var en = r * 2;
      var a = (1 + n - p) % (r * 8);
      var pos = [0, 0, r];

      switch (Math.floor(a / (r * 2))) {
        case 0:
          {
            pos[0] = a - r;
            pos[1] = -r;
          }
          break;
        case 1:
          {
            pos[0] = r;
            pos[1] = (a % en) - r;
          }
          break;
        case 2:
          {
            pos[0] = r - (a % en);
            pos[1] = r;
          }
          break;
        case 3:
          {
            pos[0] = -r;
            pos[1] = r - (a % en);
          }
          break;
      }

      return pos;
    }

    /* FIBONACCIHO SLOVA */
    container = document.getElementById("container");
    var var0 = "<div id='point'></div>";
    var var1 = "<div id='point2'></div>";
    var var3;
    var text = "";
    var i;

    /* ITERACE NEFUNGUJE PO JEDNOM CTVERECKU ALE PO KAZDEM SOUCTU JEDNICEK A NUL */
    for (var i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
      var3 = var0 + var1;
      var1 = var0;
      var0 = var3;

      text += var3;
      container.innerHTML = text;
    }

    /* VOLANI FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    var cislo = document.getElementById('container').getElementsByTagName("div");

    for(var i=0; i<cislo.length; i++) {
      cislo[i].setAttribute("class", "jedno");

      var pos = spiral(i);

      cislo[i].style.right = 300 + 40 * pos[0] + "px";
      cislo[i].style.top = 300 + 40 * pos[1] + "px";
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Te tae, te āhua me te oro
SLUG: barva-tvar-ton
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barva-tvar-ton/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barva-tvar-ton.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barva-tvar-ton/index.html
============================================================
Te tae, te āhua me te oro
 
 whero 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png | teori tae - Wassily Kandinsky]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png] 
 
E ai ki te kaitoi a Wassily Kandinsky [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasilij_Kandinskij] me tana teori tae, he hononga kei waenganui i te tae me te āhua. I whakaritea e ia nga āhua āhuahanga e toru 2D ki te tauira tae RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] i whakamahia i taua wa (whero, kōwhai, kikorangi) i roto i te whanaungatanga: tapatoru = kōwhai, tapawhā = whero, porowhita = kikorangi. I whakaaweahia ahau e tana mahi, a, i hanga e ahau he putanga hou o tenei teori tae. Engari i te tauira tae RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] e kiia ana kua tawhito inaianei, i whakamahia e ahau te tauira tae RGB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGB] (whero, kākāriki, kikorangi) he tika ake i te taha ahupūngao, a, e whakamahia nuitia ana i tenei ra. I whakakapia e ahau te tapawhā i whakamahia e Kandinsky ki te pentagon, na te mea he rite ake te tapawhā ki te tapatoru i te porowhita, i te mea ki ahau he pai ake te pentagon hei whakawhitinga i waenganui i te tapatoru me te porowhita.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png | teori tae - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png] 
 
Hei kimi i te āhua tika (pentagon) i waenganui i te tapatoru me te porowhita, i whakamahia e ahau nga koki o roto o nga āhua. Ko te koki o roto o te tapatoru rite ko 60° (ara 60^1). Ko te koki o roto o te porowhita me 180° (ara 60^1.268324336648371). Ae, karekau he taha o te porowhita, no reira karekau he koki o roto, engari hei tauira, ko te koki o roto o te mano-koki (chiliagon) ko 179,64° a he tino rite tenei āhua ki te porowhita. Ki te tatau ahau i te toharite o enei tau tohu e rua 60^[(1+1.268324336648371):2] ka whiwhi ahau i te hua 103.923°. Ko tenei koki o roto he tino rite ki te pentagon, ko tona koki o roto ko 108°. Hei whakawhānui i tenei teori tae, i whakamahia e ahau taua tikanga ano ki te taapiri i nga whakawhitinga i waenganui i nga āhua tae taketake me nga whanaungatanga: tapawhā = kōwhai, octagon = cyan. I ngana ahau ki te whakaoti i tenei teori ma te whakarite i nga āhua tae ki te teitei o te oro i runga i te whanaungatanga: koki koi + tae mahana = oro teitei, koki puhoi + tae makariri = oro hohonu. I tuhia e ahau tenei ariā ki roto i te kaimahi puoro.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png | teori tae whakawhānui - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png] 
 
I ngana ahau ki te whakaoti i tenei teori ma te whakarite i nga āhua tae ki te teitei o te oro i runga i te whanaungatanga: koki koi + tae mahana = oro teitei, koki puhoi + tae makariri = oro hohonu. I tuhia e ahau tenei ariā ki roto i te kaimahi puoro.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg | teori puoro tae - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Ko te tae anake kaore au i kaha ki te whakarite ki tetahi āhua, oro ranei, ko te magenta. Me rite ki te kākāriki (he mea ano kei waenganui i te kikorangi me te whero, he makariri, he mahana hoki i te wa kotahi). Me ahua porowhita-haurua? He tapatoru porotaka ranei? Me rite ranei ki te kākāriki - ara he pentagon ano? Tae noa ki tenei wa, kare ano au i kitea te whakautu ki tenei patai.

============================================================
TITLE: Te tirohanga a te tangata
SLUG: lidsky-zrak
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lidsky-zrak/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lidsky-zrak.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lidsky-zrak/index.html
============================================================
Te tirohanga a te tangata
 
 pipiri 2019 
 
 
 
He rerekē te tirohanga a te tangata i tā te nuinga o te iwi e whakaaro nei. He tino rerekē ngā raraunga taketake e hangaia ana e ō tātou kanohi i te āhua o te whakamāoritanga a te roro i muri mai, ā, i te āhua hoki o tā tātou whakaaro e kite ana tātou. Ko te pikitia i raro nei e whakaatu ana i ngā "raraunga mā" kua wetekina i te "whakatika-muri" a tō tātou roro.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png | te tirohanga a te tangata e whakaatahia ana]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png] 
 
I runga i te pikitia, ka taea te kite i ētahi hapa nui:
 
 
- Kua huri te ahua ki te taha whakapae me te taha poutū. Ka kite pēnei ngā pēpi katoa i muri i te whānautanga, engari i te roanga o te wā, ka ako te roro ki te whakatika i te ahua. He onge rawa te puta o tēnei pānga i muri i te kai i ngā rongoā whakaheke hinengaro.
 
- Ka whakaatuhia te ahua mō ia kanohi motuhake - mā te roro anake e hono ai hei ahua kotahi.
 
- He koi noa te ahua i tētahi wāhanga iti i waenganui, ā, ka pōuriuri haere ki ngā taha.
 
- Ko te ahua te mea tino tae i te wāhanga iti i waenganui, ā, ka ngaro te kaha o te tae ki ngā taha. Nā te nui o ngā pūtau kōnohe i waenganui o te kanohi tēnei, e heke tere ana i te pūtake. I ia kanohi he ira pango kotahi - e kīia ana he wāhi matapō (scotoma). Kāore he pūtau kōnohe, pūtau tokotoko rānei i tēnei wāhanga o te kanohi, nā te mea kei reira te io o te kanohi. I te nuinga o te wā, ka whakakīia e te roro ēnei wāhi matapō ki ngā pārongo mai i tētahi atu kanohi.
 
- Ko ngā rārangi tōtika he paku piko ki te āhua o te kōpiko, pēnei i te pānga o te arotahi ika, te arotahi whānui rānei o te kāmera. E ngana ana te roro ki te whakatika i ēnei rārangi, engari he pohewa noa iho tēnei.
 
- I roto i te āpure tirohanga, ka kitea hoki te ihu, e tātarihia ana e te roro i te nuinga o te wā.
 
- Kāore te ahua i te tino mā, engari he paku haruru tirohanga kei roto. Ka puta tēnei i te whakakotahitanga o te haruru tinana o te mārama, te haruru o ngā pūtau whakaahua, me ētahi atu mahi io o te pūnaha tirohanga. I raro i ngā tikanga noa, ka pēhia e te roro tēnei, engari ka kitea pea i te pōuri, i te wā e kati ana ngā kanohi, i runga rānei i ngā mata ōrite.
 
 
I roto i taku mahi whakamutunga o te tau tuarua o te kaupapa hanga pāpāho maha mai i te akoranga hākoritanga i te Kura Tohunga o te Hokohoko, Toi Whakamahi me te Hoahoa, i hanga e au he ataata e whakatauira ana i ngā mate kanohi rerekē e ngahau ana:
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/sTMWRaVGFIE]

============================================================
Tanzania 2019
 
Whakaahua, tuhinga
 
 
 
Ko tēnei whakaaturanga he whakarāpopototanga o ngā tirohanga me ngā mātauranga ā-roto rerekē i kohia e au i taku noho poto ki Tanzania. Ka whakapuakina e au ēnei tirohanga me ēnei kare-ā-roto mā te whakaahua me te tuhinga.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Kei te ngana ahau ki te āwhina i ngā tāngata o te rohe mā taku mahi auaha, ki te whānui ake te mōhio ki ō rātou raruraru, ā, mā reira e whakakore ai i ētahi whakapono tōmua mō te iwi. Ki tōku whakaaro hoki, me ako a Ūropi i tēnei whenua i te maha o ngā āhuatanga.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
 
E rua ngā rōpū whakapono matua i Tanzania - ko te Ihirama me te Karaitiana. Ahakoa he uaua pea te whakaaro ki te noho tahi o ēnei rōpū e rua mō te Ūropiana toharite o te rautau 21, e noho rangimārie ana ngā Mahometa me ngā Karaitiana o te rohe, ā, e whakaute ana tētahi i tētahi.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg] 

 
Ki ahau ake, kāore au i te pai ki ngā tāngata e mau kākahu ōkawa ana (ngā pirihimana, ngā hōia, ngā kaitiaki...). Inā rā, i te Czech Republic. Ki tōku whakaaro, i te piki o ngā mana i tukuna ki a rātou, ka piki hoki tō rātou whakapehapeha. Heoi, kāore tēnei whakahē a au ki ngā kākahu ōkawa i te opodstatněný.Lid i Tanzania. I ngāwari ngā tāngata i mau kākahu ōkawa ki reira ki ahau, ā, i āwhina tonu rātou i ahau.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg] 

 
He tino manaaki, he hoa te āhua o ngā tāngata o te rohe ki ahau, ahakoa i waho o ngā wāhi tūruhi. Ki te haere au ki tētahi kāinga o te rohe, ka mihi mai ngā tāngata ki te kupu "Jambo!" (Ko te tikanga "Kia ora!"). He tokomaha hoki i kōrero mai ki ahau.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg] 

 
"He nui ake te menemene o ēnei tāngata nō ngā rohe mahana i a tātou" (he wāhanga o te kōrero a ētahi kaihihi i rongo ai au i te waka rererangi i taku hokinga mai ki te Czech Republic)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Nā te ao whānui, kei te tipu te ahumahi tūruhi i Zanzibar, e mau mai ana i te maha o ngā painga, me ngā huakore hoki. Ko ngā painga matua ko te tipu o te ōhanga me te pikinga o te taumata mātauranga. Ko tētahi atu painga ko te whānui o te mōhio ki tēnei whenua i te ao, engari kāore i te pai, he maha ngā wā ka whakakorea tēnei painga e ngā pakitara nunui o ngā hōtēra, kāore nei e whitiwhitia e te tokomaha o ngā tūruhi. Ki tōku whakaaro, ko te whakangaromanga o te ahurea o te rohe he huakore. Ko tētahi tauira o tēnei raruraru ko te hekenga o te tokomaha o ngā Masai mai i te tuawhenua o Tanzania (nō te rohe huri noa i Kilimanjaro) ki ngā wāhi e nui ana te tūruhi (ki ngā takutai o Zanzibar). Ka hoko ēnei Masai i ngā taonga iti ki reira, ā, he mea mātakitaki noa rātou mā ngā tūruhi. E whakapono ana ngā Masai, ki te tangohia tētahi whakaahua o rātou, ka tahaetia tō rātou wairua. Heoi, i ngā wāhi tūruhi, ka whakaae rātou ki te tango whakaahua mō ētahi hēneti noa iho.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

Whakaahua o te tangata
 
 Hakihea 2018 
 
 
 
I whanau mai tēnei kaupapa hei mahi kura mō te kaupapa Whakaahua i te Kura Tuarua o te Tauhokohoko, Toi Whakamahi me te Hoahoa i Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Ko te whāinga he hono whakamātau i te whakaahua tangata, ngā momo āhuatanga hinengaro, te kaupapa whakaata, te hākoritanga me te oro. Ko te āhua whakamutunga o ngā whakaahua me ngā ataata i ahu mai i te whakamātautau āhuatanga o Eysenck me te wehewehenga o ngā huru i runga i te whā o Galen.

TITLE: Mātiti papa tūru
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Mātiti papa tūru
 
 Hakihea 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
I whakamahia e au he mātiti papa tūru ki te whakaahua tauira, e whai mana ana te rahi o te pouaka papa tūru kotahi ki te rahi o te pika kotahi o te whakaahua. Ko ngā mata mā o te pouaka papa tūru i muri i te whakamahinga o tēnei mātiti ko ngā pika taketake o te whakaahua, ā, ko ngā mata pango he rerekē te tae me te māramatanga. Ko te whakaahua i te rahi katoa he rite ki te mata hina kotahi te tae, engari ka kitea te whakaahua taketake i muri i te topa matihiko. Kia tika ai te whakaatu i runga i te aroturuki Full HD, me tata ki te 2000 px te teitei o te whakaahua. Ka puta pea he whakaaturanga hē ina whakatuwheratia te whakaahua i roto i te pūtirotiro e mahi ana i te tauira anō o te mātiti pika. Ko te Windows Photo Viewer tētahi pūtirotiro kua whakamatauria.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Hei tauira anō mō te whakamahinga o tēnei kaupapa, i hanga e au he whakaahua e mau ana i te rerekētanga o te tae me te tikanga.

============================================================
Te whakahuri i te mōhiohio
 
 Here-turi-kōkā 2018 
 
 
 
E hia ngā takawaenga e haere ai te tuhinga e kite nei tātou i runga i te ipurangi ki a tātou? He pono rānei tōna pono? Kia pēhea te rerekē o te ao e kite nei tātou mā roto i ngā mata o ā tātou waea, rorohiko, pouaka whakaata hoki?
 
He mea nui kia manatoko i ngā mōhiohio katoa e kite nei tātou mai i ngā puna maha, ā, kaua e whakawhirinaki ki tōna pono.
 
I hangaia tēnei mahi mā te tango i tētahi whakaahua, kātahi ka whakaahua anō i taua whakaahua kotahi i runga i te aroturuki rorohiko.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: E rua ngā rītama
SLUG: dva-rytmy
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dva-rytmy/
LANGUAGE: mi

SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dva-rytmy.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dva-rytmy/index.html
============================================================
E rua ngā rītama
 
 āperira 2018 
 
 
 
E rua ngā rītama o te ataata tuatahi: he kimo ataata me te pāwhiri oro. He paku rerekē te roa o ēnei rītama e rua. Mā tēnei rerekētanga iti, ka eke te ataata ki tētahi wāhanga o te tino raruraru. I roto i te ataata tuarua, i tāpirihia e au he rauemi whakaahua e arotahi ana ki te kaupapa whakahē i te pakanga ki tēnei ariā taketake.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/X9ms8pp89Pg?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg | ariā taketake]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg] 
E rua ngā rītama 1 - pango x mā, ariā taketake
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MQIDzwTYjoA?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg | te whakamahinga o te ariā taketake]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg]

TITLE: Ngā tau matapōkere i kōwhiria e te tangata
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Ngā tau matapōkere i kōwhiria e te tangata
 
 āperira 2018 
 
 
 
I roto i tēnei mahi, i tohaina he puka pātai ki ētahi tāngata. I roto i tēnei puka pātai, i tuhia e ngā tāngata i whakamātauria kia kotahi rau ngā tau matapōkere i roto i te awhe o 1-100, i puta mai ki ō rātou hinengaro. I muri iho, i whakawhitia e au ēnei raraunga ki te rorohiko, ā, i hangaia he kauwhata kotahi mō ia puka pātai. Ko te tuaka whakapae x e tohu ana i te raupapa o ngā tau takitahi, ā, ko te tuaka poutū y e tohu ana i te uara o ngā tau i kōwhiria. Ko te kauwhata tae kahurangi i hangaia e te rorohiko, arā, e te kaihanga tau matapōkere. I te whakatauritea ngā kauwhata katoa, ka kitea he kauwhata ahurei tō ia tangata i whakamātauria, me tōna ake tikanga ki te whakauru tau. Ka taea hoki te kite i roto i te maha o ngā kauwhata, he huringa tata ōrite e tukurua ana (te nuinga o te wā he piki haere, he heke haere rānei o ngā tau).
 
NGĀ TAU I KŌWHIRIA E TE KAIHANGA TAU MATAPŌKERE
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg | Kauwhata o ngā tau matapōkere i kōwhiria e te rorohiko]

============================================================
Te manawa kōhikohiko
 
 poutū-te-rangi 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Kei roto i tēnei ripa oro e whā meneti te roa he rekoata o tētahi ngeru e mate ana. E rua ngā oro matua e rangona ana i roto i te rekoata: he manawa kōhikohiko me tētahi oro ngāwari ake o te patu o te ngākau.

TITLE: Mai i te tirohanga a te anga
SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Mai i te tirohanga a te anga
 
 poutū-te-rangi 2018 
 
 
 
Ko enei ataata e arotahi ana ki te whakaaro o te anga, he aha tana e rongo ana, e wheako ana? Ko tana tirohanga ki te ao ehara i te mea iti iho i te tirohanga a te tangata! I te ataata tuatahi ka kitea te rekoata taketake o te moana. I te ataata tuarua, i te taha maui o te mata, ka whakaatuhia te tae toharite o ia anga o te rekoata taketake. I te taha matau o te mata he kauwhata RGB e tautuhi ana i tenei tae.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

============================================================
Pūkohu
 
 whiringa-ā-rangi 2017 
 
 
 
Ko tēnei huinga whakaahua e tuhi ana i te pūkohu i roto i taku pukapuka reo Tiamana. Kua whakaritea ngā whakaahua kia rite ki te raupapa o ngā whārangi o te pukapuka. Mā tēnei e puta mai ai ngā tito matapōkere, ngā tito whakamīharo, me ngā tito whakamātau ki ia whakaahua.

TITLE: Te whakararuraru i te mātiti auau

URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/

DATE: 2017-10-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Te whakararuraru i te mātiti auau
 
 Whiringa-ā-nuku 2017 
 
 
 
I roto i tēnei mahi, i ngana ahau ki te hanga i tētahi raupapa o ngā mahi toi waitara. I wehea e ahau te tuhinga ki ngā wāhanga e whā. I te wāhanga tuatahi, i hangaia e ahau he mātiti auau taketake, i te wāhanga tuarua, i tīmata ahau i taua mātiti nei, ā, i tīmata ahau ki te whakararuraru paku i a ia, nā reira i puta ai he āhua o te kore e noho mārie. I te wāhanga tuatoru, i whakararurarutia e ahau tēnei mātiti āhuahanga auau ki ngā āhua rauropi rerekē, kāore i te ōrite. Ā, i te wāhanga tuawhā whakamutunga, i whakahāngaihia e ahau ēnei āhua rauropi.
 
Wāhanga tuatahi:
 MĀTITI TAKETAKE
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Waitara 1.0 - mātiti auau]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg]

Te toharite tae
 
 āperira 2017 
 
 
 
Kei te taha maui o te pikitia he 1156 whakaahua i roto i te mātiti 34x34 px. Ko ia pika tae e tohu ana i te tae toharite o te whakaahua takitahi mai i taua whenua. Kei te taha matau o te pikitia te tae toharite o nga pika katoa mai i te taha maui, ara, te tae toharite o nga whakaahua katoa mai i taua whenua.

TITLE: Ngā poraka huka
SLUG: kostky-cukru

============================================================
Ngā poraka huka
 
 poutū-te-rangi 2017 
 
 
 
I hangaia te raupapa whakaahua i te wā o ngā whakamātautau taranata i te Kura Tuarua o te Tauhokohoko, Toi Whakamahi me te Hoahoa i Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Mō te wāhanga whakaahua o ngā whakamātautau, i āhei mātou ki te kawe mai i ā mātou ake tūemi, engari he iti te maha me te rahi. I kawea mai e au he pouaka poraka huka, i whakaritea e au ki runga i te pereti hei mātiti auau, kātahi ka ringihia ki te wai. I rewa haere ngā poraka, i hinga, ā, i taka matapōkeretia.

TITLE: Puna Pukapuka Toi Tiriti
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Puna Pukapuka Toi Tiriti
 
 Poutū-te-rangi 2017 
 
 
 
He maha ngā mahi toi tiriti whakamīharo e karapoti ana i a tātou, mai i ngā whakapiri iti, ngā pakitara kua peitahia, tae atu ki ngā momo 3D taonga. I te mea me noho muna ngā kaituhi o ēnei mahi nā te takahi i te ture, he ao tēnei o te toi tiriti e kore e whai hua, e motuhake ana i te ao hokohoko. Ko ngā toi tiriti e kitea ana e tātou i ngā wāhi katoa o te tāone, he mea pupuri kia hou tonu. Ko ngā toi tiriti hou e hipoki ana i ngā mea tawhito, e tihore ana te peita me te raima, ko ngā taonga 3D e whakakinongia ana, ā, ko ngā whakapiri e tihore ana, e memeha ana rānei i te pānga o te taiao i te roanga o te wā. He tukanga māori tēnei, he āhuatanga o te wā, engari ki tōku whakaaro, he toi tiriti e tika ana kia roa ake te oranga.
 
Nō reira, i whakatau ahau ki te tuhi i ngā mahi tino whakamīharo. Mō tēnei kaupapa, i te tau 2017 i whakaturia e ahau he whārangi Pukamata ko 'Street art knihovna' (Puna Pukapuka Toi Tiriti) te ingoa, ā, kei te tāpiri tonu ahau i ngā pou ki reira tae noa mai ki tēnei rā. Kia tae atu ai ki te tokomaha o ngā tāngata, i whakaturia e ahau he whārangi Instagram me taua ingoa anō.
 
Ki ēnei whārangi, e ngana ana ahau ki te tāpiri i ngā mahi e mau ana i tētahi whakaaro, ā, ki a au nei, he mea tuarua noa te āhuatanga rerehua o te mahi.
 
Puna Pukapuka Toi Tiriti (Pukamata):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Puna Pukapuka Toi Tiriti (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Whakaahua o te whārangi Pukamata]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Whakaahua o te whārangi Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
Taupānga Google
 
 Whiringa-ā-rangi 2016 
 
 
 
Ko tēnei ataata poto he tohutoro ki ngā whakaaetanga koretake o te taupānga pūkoro a Google (kua tāutahia kē te taupānga, ehara i te mea kei runga noa i ngā waea Samsung, ā, kāore hoki e taea te tango i tēnei taupānga). Ka kitea te tirohanga whānui o ngā whakaaetanga i runga i te waea e whakamahi ana i te pūnaha whakahaere Android i Nastavení/Správce aplikací/STAŽENÉ/Aplikace Google.

TITLE: Kāore he take nō hea koe i haere mai ai...
SLUG: vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2

LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: vystavy
DATE: 2016-08-14
SOURCE: src/content/posts/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2.mdx
BUILT_HTML: vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2/index.html
============================================================
Kāore he take nō hea koe i haere mai ai...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. o Hakihea 2014 i tangohia e au he whakaahua o tētahi pakitara tawhito kua tuhia ki Mombasa. Kātahi au ka ngana ki te wetewete i te tuhinga i runga i taua pakitara. I whakauruhia e au tētahi wāhanga o te tuhinga e taea ana te pānui ki roto i te pūkaha rapu ipurangi, ā, i kitea e au te whakaahua e whai ake nei.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
He pūrongo nā Plzeň TV:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

TITLE: Te Mata

============================================================
Te Mata
 
 4. 2. 2016 - 3. 4. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 Te Whare Toi o Plzeň: I MUA, INAIANEI, Ā MURI AKE 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/GALBEnzekBw?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
E hia kē te rerekē o tō tātou oranga i runga ipurangi ki tō te ao tūturu? He aha te take me puta ki waho mēnā ka taea e tātou te rapu i ngā mea katoa i runga i a Google? Ko ngā pārongo i te ipurangi he mea whakahoro, ā, he iti noa te hononga ki te mooni.
 He whakaata ēnei o ngā mea e hiahia ana tātou ki te kite.
 Ka whakaata te rā ki te mata o te wai, ka whiti ki te pakitara, ka whakaata hoki ki te whakaata… I tae mai rānei ki a tātou me te kore e panoni?
 
He kōtuitui nō te whakaaturanga:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmsbQt0g9nc
 
He kōrero anō mō te whakaaturanga i runga i te paetukutuku o Te Whare Toi o Plzeň:
 http://galerie-plzen.cz/vystavy/vystavy-2016/predtim-ted-potom

TITLE: Mai i te tāone ki te matapōkere
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/
LANGUAGE: mi
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

DATE: 2016-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Mai i te tāone ki te matapōkere
 
 Hānuere 2016 
 
 
 
I te tau 2016 i whiwhi ahau i tētahi mahi hei whakarite mō te kura toi i ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14, arā, ko te hanga i tētahi momo whakaahua o te tāone. I hiahia ahau ki te mōhio ki te tawhiti o tēnei momo whakaahua, nō reira i mahi ahau i tēnei mahi mō ngā tau e whai ake nei tae noa ki tēnei wā (te tau 2019). Mai i te whakaahua māmā o te tāone, i tae ahau ki te whakararuraru i ngā rārangi auau, he mea i puta ai he pūngao whakamīharo mai i te tirohanga toi. I tua atu, i whakamātau ahau ki te whakamahi i te matapōkere i roto i tēnei mahi, engari kāore au i kite i tētahi mea pēnei i te matapōkere tūturu kāore he take. I aukatihia ahau e te determinism i ngā wā katoa, nō reira i whakamahi noa ahau i ngā pseudomatapōkere (ka taea te whakatau i te take, engari he uaua ki te matapae i te hua, nō reira ka puta he āhua matapōkere). Ko ētahi ara i arahina ahau ki te wāhi kāore he putanga, nō reira me rapu ahau i tētahi tirohanga kē me tētahi rongoā whakairoiro kē, hei tauira, te hōtaka i te putanga ataata, te pānga moiré, te raupapa Fibonacci, me ētahi atu. Ko te raupapa o ngā pikitia i raro nei he whakarāpopototanga o ēnei mahi.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg | Mombasa: he whakaahua me ngā tuhinga a te kaituhi]

TITLE: Tarutaru

SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2008-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/plevel.mdx
BUILT_HTML: plevel/index.html
============================================================
Tarutaru
 
 Mei 2008 
 
 
 
He mahi nō te kōhungahunga.
 
Ko te mahi he tapahi i ngā puāwai e pua ana mai i te pepa tae, ā, he hanga pikitia mai i ērā.
 
Engari i te puāwai, i hanga ahau i te plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
